# Walker w9 — the hiding of SANS availability, cause by cause, with Astra's reader sweep (S6–S10)

FULL check of `claude/allavail-sans-postout-141` (D2340, D2341, D2342, D2346), 23 Nov 26, chat 152.
Build: `raptor-port/dist` as handed over (commit `ddd8c4a44`, code `d24e2406f`), served by me with `npx vite preview --port 4399`.
Brief: `docs/superpowers/briefs/2026-11-23-sans-ahead-full-walk-brief.md`; scenarios: Astra's S6–S10 with the reader sweep.

## How it was walked

- Scripts: `scripts/handpass/sah-w9-env.mjs` (port and folder), `sah-w9-lib.mjs` (the filing door, the ten readers of the
  sweep), `sah-w9.mjs` (the scenarios; `ONLY=s6d,s6p,…`), `sah-w9-report.mjs` (this table), `sah-w9-explore.mjs` (the
  world read first), `sah-w9-probe.mjs` (the bell lead below). Helpers copied from `asp-w2-lib`, `sud-w5-lib`, `sud-w6-lib`,
  `sfd-w1-lib`, `asp-w4` (restore).
- Every scenario a fresh browser context (fresh store), the clock installed before the app loads at Wed 15 Jul 26 09:00 and
  left running; later dates reached with the clock's `setSystemTime`, the page reloaded, the app's today logged (S8b, S9,
  S10). Desktop Chromium 1536×864; iPhone WebKit 402×874, `isMobile`, `hasTouch`, scale 3, real taps, a picture right after
  every tap (`taps/`) beside the settled one.
- Ranger (`bane`, member `us`/`us`, FCP pilot, CAT IP) is the man posted out as SANS, per Astra. His own demo items, read
  first (`explore.log`): Mon 13 a flight and an OFT; Thu 16 a flight 16:45–18:10 and an APPOINTMENT 17:00–18:30; Mon 20 two
  flights 08:40–11:55; Thu 23 a MEETING 10:00–11:00; Fri 17, Sat 18, Sun 19, Tue 21, Wed 22 clear. Every board fixture is
  on a clear day at clear hours (Fri 17, Sat 18, Tue 21).
- Everything made through the app's own controls: the posting (Leave War cell → ⋯ → Post out → SANS → Post out), the
  filings (Inputs → SANS → day → + Commitment → Person Ranger, Fly / OFT / AMT, Duration, Remarks, the window's calendar for
  an end date → Add), the posting's later changes (his posted-out sheet: the PO-from date box, the Posting chips, "Undo
  post out (PO)"), Quals (Pilots → ✎ Edit → his SANS cell → Done), the top bar's Undo / Redo, Admin → Users (Archive;
  archived list → Post in → Restore), board fixtures (+ Wave → Flying wave; + on OFT; + Item with ALL AVAIL).
  `window.*` was only READ (INPUTS, PEOPLE, WARN, ARM, CURWEEK, CURPAGE), never written.
- **The reader sweep (Astra's table), at every stage — active, hidden, restored:** (1) the SANS month's F / O / A figures
  and still-needed pair, judged against the active stage (one pilot fewer for each offered letter when hidden); (2) each
  SANS day opened — his row by the filing's own id, in any group (an apart-list included), its "SANS committed to fly"
  pilots agreeing with the month's F; (3) Inputs → List — no row for a SANS filing (by design), his ordinary inputs
  listed; (4) Edit Schedule's crew panel stepped to the day; (5) the Scheduler Board's crew list — SANS Avail with the
  badge, or an ordinary pilot with none; (6) seats armed — a Fly seat, an OFT seat outside his offered hours; (7) the
  ALL AVAIL window of a Common Programme row (RECORD); (8) the flying-plan figures (= reader 2's agreement with the month);
  (9) the warning list (window.WARN read: every warning naming Ranger); (10) his bell, signed in as him in the same tab.
- Each read was calibrated by the walk itself: the same read gives the active value at stage A and the hidden value at
  stage B (e.g. "SANS — not offering Fly" at A, nothing at B, the same seat).

## Runs (one folder each; the cited run of each scenario named) — all under `D:\raptor-work\c152-walk\w9\`

| Scenario | Desktop (cited run) | Phone (cited run) |
|---|---|---|
| S6 | `desk2/` | `dbg3/` |
| S7, S8, S8b (S8 after the posting has run), S10 | `dbg4/` | `phone2/` |
| S9 | `desk2/` | `phone3/` |

Earlier attempts, NOT cited: `dbg1/` (my bell read did not know the badge reads "RANGER · MEMBER" — the script stopped
signed in as Ranger), `dbg2/` (S6 desktop: five "day" FAILs that were my script comparing "F · O · A" with "F/O/A"; every
other step passed — re-run as `desk2/`), `phone1/` (on a phone the SANS day's window stays open after a save and covers
the month: my script did not close it before the second filing — fixed, re-run as `phone2/`), `dbg4/` S9 desktop and
`phone2/` S9 phone (my sweep signed out to read his bell between the Quals untick and Undo; a sign-out clears the session's
Undo by design (D148), so Undo was greyed — the script stopped; re-run with the bell read after Redo, as `desk2/` and
`phone3/`). The deciding pictures are copied to `docs/img/handpass/2026-11-23-sans-ahead/w9/` (list at the end).

## Result in short

**876 rows (both sizes): PASS 702 · FAIL 0 · RECORDED 118 · NOT WALKED 6 · NOTE 50** (a NOTE is a read logged with no
judgement: the app's today, the active stage's figures). Desktop and phone gave the same result on every step.
Errors: **none** — no console error, page error, failed request or 4xx in any cited run (nor in the earlier attempts).
Pictures: 2,196 in the cited run folders on `D:` (every phone tap with its picture within ~100 ms in `taps/`); 44 deciding
pictures copied to the repo (list at the end).

Every hiding cause did what D2341 says, on every reader, at both sizes:

| Cause | Hidden (stage B) | Back (stage C) |
|---|---|---|
| **S6** posting moved 17 → Mon 20 (posted-out sheet, PO-from date box; line "On 20 Jul: becomes SANS.") | 17, 18, 19: gone from the month (F/O/A 1 0 → 0 0), from the opened day (no row in any group, "No commitments yet."), from both crew lists (an ordinary pilot under PILOTS, no badge); 20, 21 kept (SANS Avail, badge "F/O/A") | moved back 20 → 17: all five days back by themselves, the one record unchanged (Fly/OFT/AMT, All day, "W9 S6 offer", filer Saber), no duplicate |
| **S7** "Undo post out (PO)" on the posted-out sheet | 17 (OFT 09:00–10:00) and 20 (Fly) gone everywhere; the OFT seat 14:00–15:00 and the Fly seat lose "SANS — available 09:00–10:00 only" / "SANS — not offering Fly"; the sheet closes, the Leave War row shows no PO | posted out as SANS from 17 again (cell → ⋯ → Post out → SANS → Post out): both back, the refusals back, records unchanged |
| **S8** the outcome away from SANS: the posted-out sheet's **Posting chips — the chosen "✓ SANS" tapped again** leaves no chip chosen; line "On 17 Jul: off the manpower, nothing else." | as S7 | "SANS" tapped again: "On 17 Jul: becomes SANS.", all back unchanged |
| **S8b** the same once the posting has RUN (clock Mon 20) | the chips work the same on a run posting; his record goes from SANS-from-17 to not SANS; 20 and 21 hidden | SANS again from 17; both back |
| **S9** Quals untick on Mon 20 | 20, 21, 23 hidden; **18 kept** (his earlier SANS period not erased) | Undo ("Undid: Ranger's quals") brings all back; Redo ("Redid: Ranger's quals") hides them again; a new tick on Thu 23 (SANS from 23) brings back 23 only — 20 and 21 stay hidden (the gap), 18 kept |
| **S10** archive on Mon 20, restore Wed 22 with post-in 22 | 20, 21, 22 absent everywhere (he is off the crew lists while archived) | after the restore he is **not SANS** (see R3), so 20, 21, 22 stay hidden — nothing filled into the archived days |

The bell (signed in as Ranger in the same tab): after each posting take-back his bell says, word for word,
"Your SANS availability for Fri 17, Sat 18, Sun 19 Jul is hidden while you're not SANS those days." (S6) and "Your SANS
availability for Fri 17, Mon 20 Jul is hidden while you're not SANS those days." (S7, S8), under a struck
"~~You're SANS from Fri 17 Jul.~~" and "taken back · Saber"; never a day still filed and shown (S6: never 20 or 21).
After a Quals untick, **no** such line (S9 — PASS as the host's EXPECTED, as built). Archive / restore writes none (S10).

## Every FAIL

None.

## Leads and RECORDED points the host should read (each "to check", none filed as a fault)

- **R1 — his SANS-days bell line is missing until the week of his planned days has been EDITED** (a lead; the line is
  w8's S15, met here in my sweep). Fresh world, Wed 15: Ranger posted out as SANS from Fri 17; he is planned on Mon 20
  (two demo flights) and Thu 23 (MEETING) with nothing filed. His bell: **no line** — not after the save, not after
  Saber opens the week of 20 Jul, not after filings for 17 / 20 / 21. After ONE edit in that week (a wave added on Tue
  21) his bell at once says "You're SANS from Fri 17 Jul. You're planned on Mon 20, Thu 23 Jul with nothing filed. Tap
  to file." Same cause explains why S6's walk had the line (its fixtures edited that week) and S7's had none. The brief's
  EXPECTED says the line comes "from the moment the posting is saved". Probable reading: the bell reads only weeks
  already saved, and a demo week never edited is not saved — if so, D56 may apply (the demo seed), the host to judge.
  Script `sah-w9-probe.mjs` (v2, `SHORT=1`), run `D:\raptor-work\c152-walk\w9\probe3\`; pictures
  `probe-p1b-after-week20-opened-bell-desktop.png` (no line) and `probe-p1c-after-an-edit-in-week20-bell-desktop.png`
  (the line).
- **R2 — the take-back line strikes a line he never had** (RECORD; w10's S16 owns the read/unread case). In S7 and S8
  his bell had no SANS-days line before the take-back (R1), yet the take-back line still shows the struck heading
  "~~You're SANS from Fri 17 Jul.~~ · taken back · Saber" before the hidden sentence (`s7-s7-B-bell-desktop.png`,
  `s7-s7-B-bell-phone.png`). The code's own comment says the whole line is struck and only the DAYS depend on his having
  read it, so this is as built; whether a man should be told a line was "taken back" that he never saw is a question.
- **R3 — archive wipes his SANS status; restore does not bring it back** (S10, RECORD). After Archive on Mon 20 his
  record reads not SANS, no SANS date; after Restore (post-in Wed 22, note "Ranger restored — he can sign in, and he'll
  be asked to check his quals and CAT") still not SANS. So the filings for 20–22 are kept (records unchanged) but stay
  hidden; they would come back only once someone makes him SANS again. Astra: "Inspect his restored SANS status" — this
  is that status. (`s10-s10-08-archived-desktop.png`, `s10-s10-10-restored-desktop.png`, `s10-s10-C-month-desktop.png`.)
- **R4 — the archive reaches back to a day BEFORE it** (S10, RECORDED). Archived on Mon 20, his Sat 18 filing (a day he
  WAS SANS, not published) changes: the SANS month's Sat 18 F goes 1 0 → 0 0, and the opened Sat 18 lists him under a
  "NOT COUNTED · 1" group with the reason "archived" (`s10-s10-B-day-18-desktop.png`, `…-phone.png`); his crew
  lists on 18 no longer show him. Astra: "the absence between archive and restore must not be filled retrospectively";
  the brief: a hidden offer is absent "including from any 'no longer SANS' apart-list" — 18 is not a D2341 hiding (he
  was SANS that day), it is the archive applied to a past day. The host to judge against People & accounts' archive
  rulings.
- **R5 — ALL AVAIL membership** (RECORD, every cause): with his offer active he is OUT of the event's ALL AVAIL (a SANS
  man not named — 44 people on Sat 18 in S6); hidden because he is no longer SANS he is IN (45); back, OUT (44). S9 the
  same (36 / 37 / 36 / 37, and IN on Tue 21 after the re-tick from 23). S8 and S8b (the posting "off the manpower") keep
  him OUT. S10 archived: OUT. So the membership follows his dated status, never the hidden offer, as Astra asks.
- **R6 — warnings**: at no stage does a warning naming Ranger mention SANS; the ones naming him are his demo flights'
  clashes (Mon 13, Thu 16; Mon 20 "Tight turn … VL BFM → VL ACM", "2 people are double turning: Ranger, Echo"),
  the same before and after each hiding.
- **R7 — "LATE"**: every filing made on Wed 15 for 17 Jul onward carries the LATE tag on the SANS day (the late cut-off
  as set) — expected by the cut-off, noted only.

## What could not be walked, and why

- **His bell while archived** (S10 stage B, both sizes): an archived man's sign-in is suspended ("Ranger archived — his
  sign-in is suspended"), so his bell cannot be opened — NOT WALKED.
- **His bell between the Quals untick and Undo / Redo** (S9 stages B and C, both sizes): reading his bell needs a sign-out,
  which clears the session's Undo list by design (D148) — the first attempt proved it (Undo greyed). His bell was read
  after Redo (stage D, the same untick: no line — PASS) and after the re-tick (stage E) — NOT WALKED at B and C.
- **"Historical published days keep their issued record"** (S10): no day was published in my S6–S10 (Astra's S11, the
  causes after publication, is w10's).
- Everything else of S6–S10 was walked at both sizes.

## The lessons of §7.11 — which step covers each, or why it cannot apply

| # | Lesson (short) | Covered by / why not |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | state × every reading | The reader sweep: each cause (posting moved, Undo post out, outcome off SANS — before and after the run, Quals untick / Undo / Redo / re-tick, archive / restore) × ten readers × active / hidden / restored, both sizes. |
| 2 | drags and swipes at speed | Not applicable: no drag or swipe in these scenarios (the filing's end date is a tap on the window's calendar). |
| 3 | what tells him it happened, what is out of sight | Each change's own words read (the sheet's line "On 20 Jul: becomes SANS.", "off the manpower, nothing else.", the Undo / Redo notes "Undid: Ranger's quals", the archive / restore notes) and every out-of-sight place read by the sweep (other days, the other week, his bell). |
| 4 | a window that leaves the page working | The SANS day window: the month behind it read with it open (dayRead's picture shows both; the figures agree). On the phone the window covers the month — closed by its ✕ before the next step (as a person does). |
| 5 | a box moved into sight, checked at rest | His crew puck clipped where it rests in the panel / board drawer after scrolling into view (`…-clip-…` pictures). |
| 6 | handed to the phone (scroll, keys, bounce) | Not exercised: no keyboard-driven or scroll-driven change here; the remarks box typed by fill. Named for his look: none new. |
| 7 | steered frame by frame | Not applicable: nothing animated by the change. |
| 8 | approved drawing | Not my scope: the only drawing (D2346's fade) is w8's (S19–S21); none of S6–S10 has a drawing. |
| 9 | lists / tables measured | Not measured: no list layout changed by this work; the F/O/A figures read per day, both sizes. |
| 10 | page hidden and shown again | Not applicable to S6–S10 (S20's midnight with the page open is w8's). |
| 11 | reading depending on fit | Not applicable: the readings here do not depend on what fits. |
| 12 | every page a window opens from | The posted-out sheet opened from his PO cell (17 or 20) and from the bid window's ⋯ → Post out; the SANS day from the month. Other doors are the plain-user round (w8). |
| 13 | pinch | Not applicable. |
| 14 | keyboard walk's boxes | Not applicable: no keyboard walk in S6–S10. |
| 15 | bars over a scrolling area | Not applicable: no bar changed. |
| 16 | after a refusal at a box | No refusal arose in S6–S10 (every filing was on a SANS day); the refusal is S4 (w8). |
| 17 | shows / hides part of a page | The board's AIRCREW drawer (phone) and the crew panel tab opened and closed each time it was read. |
| 18 | print / download | Not applicable. |
| 19 | every window closed each way | The SANS day window closed by ✕; the posted-out sheet by ✕; the bell by Escape. Every closing way of each is the plain-user round (w8). |
| 20 | a gesture the ways a hand makes it | Not applicable: taps only. |
| 21 | the test browser's touch is not his phone's | Named: the phone taps are WebKit emulation; nothing in S6–S10 depends on touch-only behaviour. |
| 22 | checked by what is drawn | Every reading is of drawn elements (boxes wider than 1px): the figures, the rows, the badge beside the puck, the printed reason, the bell's lines; pictures opened for the deciding steps. |
| 23 | what a tap opens, per the rulings | The bell's tap is w8's (S15); here the tap on his PO cell opens the posted-out sheet (as built, D229/D300); on the bid window ⋯ → Post out. |
| 23b | side-by-side boxes one height and line | Not measured (no layout change); seen in the pictures, nothing out of line. |
| 24 | placed against the top bar | Not applicable. |
| 25 | made through the app's own controls in every place | The filings made through + Commitment on each day (one run of days, single days, Fly-only, OFT-only with hours, AMT-only, F/O/A); seats of three kinds armed (flying, OFT); ALL AVAIL on a Common Programme row. |
| 26 | gesture measured by what is seen | Not applicable. |
| 27 | neighbours of a changed control | Not applicable: no control changed size. |
| 28 | what a press opens proved on screen (phone) | The phone's posted-out sheet, SANS day window and bell pictured on screen as opened, before anything in them was pressed (`taps/` and the settled pictures). |
| 29 | anything that reads today, past midnight | Days crossed by the clock's `setSystemTime` with a reload (S8b, S9 → Mon 20 and Thu 23, S10 → Mon 20 and Wed 22); the page left open across midnight is S20 (w8). |
| 30 | every phone tap pictured within ~100ms | `taps/*-now-phone.png` for every press, beside the settled pictures. |
| 31 | a fitted page scrolling inside itself at 125% | Not applicable: no fitted page changed. |
